athāto rahasyam vakṣye samyaksamādhilakṣaṇam
śrīherukam sthitam yena sādaram nānya vidyate //1//
samvartyādi vivartyantam sūcanā nirmāṇam sphuṭam
dharmasambhogādi hy atra sarvam piṇḍīkṛtam bhavet //2//
prathamam sthānam āsādya vijane parvatādiṣu
śmaśānam kārayet tatra bali tatkṣetrapālakān //3//
arghādy ādāpayed yogi mukhaśaucādipūrvakam
bhagavān mūrtim ālambya paṭādibhāvanoktavān //4//
pretāsanamahāghoram bhāvanā jagadarthavān
samādhim ālambayec ca jhaṭityā sarvasarvakam //5//
śrīkāra sarvabhūtārtham bāhyañ cādhyātmakam matam
ete samvṛtirūpañ ca vivṛtir yat sukhāntakam //6//
sarvam
sthānam āsādya

hekāra hṛdayāt sarvān herukādisvacakragān //7//
utpadyanti dharādyāś ca bhāvanāsarvasambhavān
sa śūnyahetvātmakañ ca na hetvādyātmakalpanā //8//
svabhāvadharmanairātmyam sa śūnyam yogavāhinī
vyūham pudgalanairātmyam kalpanājālakam mahat //9//
rukāra pratyayātmañ ca sa vyūhāpagatam mama
kaḥ punarvādam akhilam jñānādvaiteṣu yā mayā //10//
na kvacit sthitam yasmāc ca tasmāt kakārakam yataḥ
na bhāvapratyakṣaśūnyam vastumātrasurūpakam //11//
śrīherukapadan devi bhāvanā sarvagam purā
hṛdi sūryābjamadhyeṣu jñānabījam svayambhuvam //12//

suvarṇakārī lohārī maṇihārī tu dāvakī / ḍombī naṭī kapālinī kaivartī veņunaṭī śaṁkhinī tantuvāpī kandunī kāṣṭhakārikā mālinī tailinī cchepī kośakārī dhūtinī haḍagāḍī gaņikā kallavālī kūparī rājabhaṭī khaṭṭikī tambolavikrayīva suvarṇakārī lohārī maṇihārī dāvakī mlecchi oḍinī vaṇijī pattharagāḍhī kṛṣikān //90//

prabhākarī bhūmiś caiva pūjanīyā guruḥ svayam /42 svanāmoccāraṇamantrais tu praṇavādyā prakīrtitāḥ //93//

suvarṇakārī lohārī maṇihārī dāvakī mlecchi oḍinī vaṇijī pattharagāḍhī kṛṣikān carmakārī varṇa cakreṣu tesu taḥ† śeṣam draṣṭavyā hṛdaya-
kṣetram atra -sarvakulātmakām dvitīya-
āhuḥ svakam svayam bhūmiś caiva pūjanīyā
pūjanīya svanāmoccāraṇa- -mantrais -dyā prakīrtitāḥ -kārānteṣv kārayet -bāhye vartulan dadyāt -rekhā- dvāra- -vedī hārā- -śobhitām
dvāre madhye ca kākāsyā ḍākinī yataḥ uttare ulūkāsyā tu paścime śvānavaktrikā // dakṣine sūkarāsyā tu varṇam lāmādikā parā // agninaiṛtyavāyavye īśāne koṇavāsinī
yamadāḍhi dūtī damṣṭrī mathanī yathākramāt // dvau dvau varṇasamā kāryā mukhānurūpataḥ kramāt
pūrvottarapaścime tu dakṣine bhūmayaḥ sadā // kṛṣṇanīlaharidraktā pītavarṇā tu kārayet
sahajamandalam evam yasmāc cakracatusṭayam // putam dharmasambhoganirmāṇam paścāt kāryā yathākramāt
dvitīyacakram trirekham tṛtīyañ caturekhakam // caturtham pañcarekhātmā śmaśānam sarvataḥ kramāt
dakṣine sūkarāsyā varṇam -lāmādikā parā agni- -vāyavye īśāne koṇa- yamadāḍhī dūtī damṣṭrī mathanī -nurūpataḥ kramāt pūrvottara- -nīla- -harid- -raktā pītavarṇā sahaja- -maṇḍalam cakra- -caṭuṣṭayam putam -nirmāṇam paścāt kāryā -kramāt dvitīyacakram trirekham catu- -rekhakam pañcarekhātmā śmaśānam sarvataḥ vartulākārakam kuryād bāhyādibhūmikam matam // pañcarekhātmakam cakrañ catuskoṇam samujjvalam
tatra sahajacakrasya śmaśānāni ca kathyate // candogragahvaram caiva jvālākulakarankakam
vibhīṣaṇaś ca pūrvādi dikṣu vāmena samsthitam // aṭṭaṭṭahāsa īśānyā lakṣmīvana hutāśane
ghorāndhakāro naiṛtyām vāyavyāṁ kilikilāravaḥ // śmaśānāni ghorarūpāṇi vetāḍabhūtaśivāravai
anena krameṇa tisthet asta śmaśānāni ca // vṛkṣadikpālanāgendrā meghendrāś ca punaḥ kramāt
śirīṣāśvatthakankeli cūtavṛkṣavaṭas tathā // karañjakañ caiva latāparkaṭis tu hi pārthivaḥ

kāyavākcittadharmeşu dharmacakrasuśobhanam /
śmaśānāni hi pathyante agnicakreṣu madhyataḥ // māraṇam samtrāsanañ ca mahābhayabhayamkaram /
raudraśmaśānakañ caiva vāmāvarteṣu vinyaset // uccāṭakam vidveṣaṇam mūkanaṁ stambhanam punaḥ /
īśānādiṣu koņe ca vṛkṣā kramas tu dāpayet // pūgākṣoṭakanālīradāḍimabilvakas tataḥ /
āmalabijjorarudra yathākramā tu vinyaset // indri yamā rudrā ca yakṣiṇī bhūtinī ṛṣī /
rākṣasī vāyubhāryā ca dāpayel lokapālinī // nāginī meghinī sarvām dāpayet tatra sarvathāḥ /
evam maṇḍalacakrākhyam kārayet sarvasampadām /
bhāvayen nāyakan tatra karmānurūpavarṇakām //

sarvathāḥ kārayet -nurūpa- -varṇakām iti tṛtīyamedinīcakram //
mandalaiḥ saha dvitīyapuṭam //
atha bāhyato punar atra agnicakram vadāmy aham/
raktavarṇam mahādīptam ṣaṭtrimśāreṣu parām //
yoginī vinyasec cāpi devādiṣu kulodbhavām /
devinī nāginī yakṣī bhūtī ca bhāvamātrakam //
kin tat sarvam ity āha kathyate devakulodbhavām /
mātā bhāryā bhaginī ca duhitā bhāgineyikā //
pitu bhagini tśnu† sā ca mātulasya bhāryakā /
bhāryābhaginī mātā ca tasyaiva pitu mātṛkā //
tṛtīya- -medinī- -cakram dvitīya- -varṇam -dīptam ṣaṭtrimśā- -śāreṣu parām yogini vinyasec cāpi -dbhavām sarvam -lodbhavām mātā bhāryā bhaginī duhitā bhāgineyikā pitu bhaginī sā ca mātulasya bhāryakā bhāryābhaginī mātā ca tasyaiva pitu mātṛkā bhāryāpitāmahī mātu mātā ca bāndhavī/
mātu bhaginī bhāgineyikā //
svamātu mātā bhaginī bhāgineyī 'sya putrikā /
pitur mātā pitāmahī †bhi† pitulasya bhāryakā //
duhitāputrabhāryā tu bhāryāyā bhaginī punaḥ /
svapitur bhaginī putri tasyaiva tu svagotrajā //
bhrātāyā bhāryā putri ca putrasyaiva tu bhāryakā duhitāyā bhartṛmātuḥ putrasyaiva tu svasṛkā //
bhāryāpitāmahī mātu mātā bāndhavī mātu bhaginī bhāgineyikā svamātu mātā bhaginī bhāgineyī 'sya putrikā pitur mātā pitāmahī †bhi† pitulasya bhāryakā duhitāputrabhāryā bhāryāyā bhaginī punaḥ svapitur bhaginī swaḥ pi tu rbha gi nī ye , pitu bhaginīye and pitu bhaginī , and svapitur bhaginī ◊ (bu mo ); cf. ba dzra pu trī ye , pitu putrīye and pitu putrī , and putrī◊ tasyaiva tu svagotrajā ]
(de nyid kyi ni rang rigs skyes ); cf. ba dzra ta syai ba tu swa rgo tra dzī ye , tasyaiva tu gotrajā .

(bu mo ); cf. ba dzra pu trī ye , bhātrā putriye and bhrātrā putrī , and pitrī ◊ putrasyaiva tu bhāryakā ]

duhitāyā bhartṛmātuḥ putrasyaiva tu svasṛkā ]
corr.; duhitāyā bhattṛmātuḥ putrasyaiva tu svaśṛkā A; duhitāyā bhattṛmātuḥ putrasyaiva tu svaśṛkā B; duhitāyā bhartumātuḥ putrasyaiva tu svaśṛkā C; duhitāyā bhattṛmātuḥ putrasaiva tu svaśṛkā D; bu mo’i khyo yi ma yi ni bu nyid kyi ni sgyug mo (Only the last word sgyug mo is not equivalent); cf. ba dzra du hi tā ya bhā rtri mā tuḥ pu tra syai ba duhitāputrī samākhyātā ṣaṭtrimśatidūtikā /
raktavarṇā samākhyātā āyudhādi ca pūrvavat //

parīttābhāpramāṇābhī Vābhāsurī parīttaśubhī apramāṇaśubhī subhakṛtsnā anabhrakī punyaprasavā bṛhatphalacakravartini avṛhī Vatapī sudṛśī sudarśanī akaniṣṭhavartinī ākāśānantyāyatanī vijñānānantyāyatanī ākiñcanyāyatanī naivasamjñānāsamjñānī nārakī pretanī tathā tiryā narī asuri vimānacāriṇī tathā ākāśānatyāyatanī vijñānānantyāyatanī ba dzra bi dznyā nā na ntyā ya ta na tsa kra ba rti nī ye, vijñānānantyāyatanā ākiñcanyāyatanī akiñcinyāyatanī; ākimcintyāyatanī; akimcanyāyatanī; ba dzra a ka nytsi nyā ya ta na tsa kra ba rti ni ye, ākimcinyāyatanī naivasamjñānāsamṁjñānī ba dzra nai ba sam dznyā nā sam dznyā ya ta na tsa kra ba rti nī ye, naivasamjñānāsamjñāyatanī, naivasamjñānāsamjñānī nārakī ba dzra na ra ka tsa kra ba rti ni ye, nārakī nārakā pretanī ma pretavī ba dzra pre ta tsa kra ba rti nī ye, pretanī pretakī tathā tiryā ba dzra ti rya ka tsa kra ba rti nī ye, niryā tiryyī narī na ba dzra na ra tsa kra ba rti ni ye, nakī narī asurī min asucī ba dzra a su ra tsa kra ba rti nī ye, asurī asūrī vimānacāriṇī śaśī ravi yamā indrī vijñeyā cakravartinī /675

/rgya gar skad du/ shrī ḍā kā rṇa ba ta ntra ma ṇḍa la tsa kra sya sā dha na ra tna pa dma rā ga ni dhi nā ma/
bod skad du/dpal mkha' 'gro rgya mtsho'i rgyud kyi dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo'i sgrub thabs rin po che pa dma rā ga'i (D 1v2) gter zhes bya ba/
dpal bcom ldan 'das mkha' 'gro rgya mtsho la phyag 'tshal lo/
/gang zhig sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/
/thugs rjes kun bsdus ngo bo'i sku/
/dngos kun gcig bsdus rang bzhin (D 1v3) te/
/rgyud kyi phyag can lhan skyes zhal//
/gnyis med shes rab phag mo dang//
lhan cig bzhugs pa de la 'dud/
/mkhyen pa'i gsung gi´od zer gyis//
snod ldan blo mun sel mdzad cing//
/(D 1v4) don gyi bdud rtsis tshim mdzad pa/
/mtsho skyes la sogs bla ma'i tshogs/
/zla ba lta bu rnams la 'dud//
/dpal ldan mkha' 'gro rgya mtsho yi/
/bla ma'i man ngag mngon rtogs rim/
(1v1) and sgrub thabs rin po che D (colophon, 35r7).
/ji bzhin rtogs (D 1v5) pa'i nus med kyang//
bdag nyid dran phyir bri bar bya//
de la thog mar dpal mkha' 'gro rgya mtsho'i dbang bskur ba'i rim pas dag par byas pa zab pa dang rgya che ba la lhag par mos pas rgyud kyi don khong du chud (D 2r1)pa'i sgrub pa pos/ skye bo med pa'i sa phyogs dang/ri bo dang/ ri'i phug dang/bya skyibs dang/ rgya mtsho chen po'i 'gram dang/gdod ma nas grub pa'i dur khrod dang/ gang du yid dga' ba'i gnas su yang rung ste/bcom ldan 'das kyi (D 2r2) ras ris dang/ gtor ma dang/ mchod yon la sogs pa'i nye bar spyad pa rnams dang ldan pa’i bsam gtan gyi gnas bstar ba/ bdud rtsis gtor zhing/ me tog gcal du bkram pas dga' bar byas par/ tho rangs sad pa dang rdo rje rnal 'byor ma rnams kyis glu dang cang te'u'i sgras bskul bar bsams te/gdong gi gtsang sbra la sogs byas sgrub pa pos gcal du nas/ ro dngos kyi steng ngam mi’i pags pa la sna tshogs rdo rje bris pa'i steng du/ gcer bu khar bdud rtsi'i ri lu bcug pa lhor mngon par phyogs pas/ rdo rje´i skyil krung la sogs pas 'dug ste/ lag pa g-yon pa'i dbus dang mthe'u chung nas bzung ba'i sor mo rnams kyi rtsa ba la dpa' mo'i go cha rnams kyis sbyang ba dang/bcom ldan 'das kyi go cha yang dbus dang sor mo rnams kyi rtse mo la dgod do//

bgyis  yi rangs 210 de nas snying rje dang byams pa dang dga' ba dang btang snyoms dran par bya'o//
de'i rjes su/ om sarvadharmāḥ śūnyasvabhāvāḥ śūnyo 'ham/ om [svabhāvaśuddhāḥ] sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho 'ham/ om śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako ‘ham/ om yogaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmā yogaśuddho 'ham/ zhes bya ba brjod cing don dran par byas te/ chos thams cad nam mkha’i dkyil ltar bsgom par bya'o//
211 yoga—with [this] recitation, having been mindful of [their] meanings, he should meditate on all phenomenal existences having the sky-like nature (emptiness).
de nas sngon gyi smon lam gyis sad pa'i sems nam mkha' la nā da la rim gyis rdzogs pa'i hum blta'o//
de dang dus mnyam du bsrung ba'i ra ba la sogs pa rnams kyang blta bar bya'o//
de'i nang gi 'og gi char yam sngon po yongs su gyur pa las rlung gi dkyil 'khor sngon po gzhu'i dbyibs lta bu mtha' gnyis na g-yo bzhin pa'i ba dan yam gis byin gyis brlab po//
D 5r2-r6 → Dākārṇava, 15.16. Inside that [enclosure], at the bottom [the letter] yam in dark blue [is produced]; [yam is] transformed into the wind wheel, [which is] dark blue, shaped like a bow, [equipped with] swinging flags on both ends, and blessed (marked) with yam.
de'i steng du ram dmar po yongs su gyur pa las me'i dkyil 'khor dmar po gru gsum pa 'bar bzhin pa'i grwa can ram yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa'o//
de'i steng du bam dkar po yongs su gyur pa las chu'i dkyil 'khor dkar po zlum po bum pa dang bcas pa la bam yig go//
212 de'i steng du lam ser po yongs su gyur pa las/ sa'i dkyil 'khor ser po gru bzhi pa grwar rdo rje rtse gsum pa dang ldan pa lam yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa'o//

dmar po] D; dmar ba P. de nas yab kyi gsang ba'i rdo rje mi dmigs pa las hūm sngon po las rdo rje sngon po rtse Inga pa'o//

The third circle (Earth Circle) is thus .
The second layer comprising Circles.
/de nas phyi rol slar yang 'dir/
/me yi 'khor lo bdag gis bshad/

/lha sogs rigs las byung ma ste//
Now, here outside, another, I shall explain the Fire Circle, is red in color and is blazing greatly.
He should arrange yoginīs born of the gods' clan and others on the excellent thirty-six spokes.

This is iti tṛtīya medinīcakram / maṇḍalaiḥ saha dvitīyapuṭam/ in the Sanskrit ṇākārṇava (–).

→ Dākārṇava .
[The yoginis are] (1) Devī, (2) Nāgī, (3) Yakṣiṇī, (4) Bhūtinī, and the solely existing.
[The goddess] asked Is that all?
[Then, those] born of the gods' clan are stated [by the Blessed One as follows]—(5) Mātā (mother), (6) Bhāryā (wife), (7) Bhaginī (sister), (8) Duhitā (daughter), (9) Bhāgineyikā (sister's daughter), (10) Pitur Bhaginī (father's sister), (11) Sā ca Mātulasya Bhāryaki chung ma yi ni ma dang ni ] D; om. P. phyi mo ] D; phyi ma P. sbun zla'i ] D; sbun zla sbun zla'i P. de nyid kyi ] D; de nyid kyis P. bu mo dang ] D; om. P. bu de yi ni ] D; om. P.
Her husband's name (a masculine form of her name) is Māta according to his mantra.
Therefore, it seems that her name, mātā, is used here as an ā-stem noun and not a nominative of mātṛ.
This is also said of all words of mātā mentioned below that are used as yoginīs' names.
Her husband's name (a masculine form of her name) is Duhita according to his mantra.
Her name, duhitā, is an ā-stem noun and not a nominative of duhitṛ.
This is also said of all duhitās mentioned below that are used as yoginīs' names.
(and maternal uncle's that wife), (12) Bhāryābhaginī (wife's sister), (13) Bhāryāmātā (wife's mother), (14) Tasyaiva Pitur Mātṛki (her [viz., wife's] father's mother; tasyaiva is tasyā eva), (15) Bhāryāpitāmahī (wife's paternal grandmother), (16) Mātur Mātā (mother's mother), (17) Bāndhavī (female relative), (18) Mātur Bhaginī (mother's sister), (19) Bhāgineyikā (sister's daughter), (20) Svamātur Mātābhaginī (one's own mother's mother's sister), (21) Bhāgineyī (sister's daughter”).
(22) Asya Putrikā (her [viz., sister's daughter's] daughter; asya is asyāḥ), (23) Pitur Mātā (father's mother”), (24) Pitāmahī (paternal grandmother”), (25) Pitulasya Bhāryakī (paternal uncle's wife), (26) Duhitāputrabhāryī (daughter's son's wife), and again (27) Bhāryāyā Bhaginī (wife's sister), (28) Svapitur Bhaginī (one's own father's sister), (29) Putrī ([one's own father's] daughter), (30) Tasyaiva tu Svagotrajī (that same one's [viz., one's own father's] own kinswoman), (31) Bhrātāyā Bhāryā (brother's wife; bhrātāyā[ḥ] is bhrātur), (32) Putrī (“[brother's] daughter”), (33) Putrasyaiva tu Bhāryaki (the same son's wife), (34) Duhitāyā Bhartṛmātuḥ Putrasyaiva tu Svasṛkā (daughter's husband's mother's same son's sister), (35) Duhitā (daughter), and (36) Putrī (daughter).
The thirty-six female messengers (yoginīs) are made known.
proclaimed to be red in color, and physical features such as weapons are as before.

Vāsanottīrṇagati, Abhedyabuddhi, Udadhi, Sodhita, Ganimuktirāja, Priyābha, Bodhidhvaja, Jñānaratna, Suśītala, Brahmarāja, He is named Śodhita (for dag par mdzad pa) (perhaps a conjectural emendation) in (Dharma p. ), and Vimārṣṭa in the Sarvavajrodayā (Moriguchi , p. ).

Ratibala, Sughoṣa, Dharmeśvara, Brahmaruta, Suceṣṭa, Askhalitabuddhi, Mahāpraṇāda, Yaśaḥkīrti, Ketumat, Vighusṭatejas, 'gro ba'i dbang phyug dang/ ljon pa dang/ gti mug rab tu zad pa dang/ dpag tu med pa dang/ zla ba bzang po dang/ spobs pa mtha' yas tog dang/ brtul zhugs brtan pa dang/ mchod pa'i gnas dang/ mya ngan rgal ba dang/ dge ba dgyes pa dang/
Jagadīśvara, Druma, Supraṇastamoha, Amita, Sucandramas, Anantapratibhānaketu, Vratanidhi, Pūjya, Uttīrṇaśoka, Kṣemapriya, 'gro ba'i blo gros dang/ dgyes par gshegs dang/ zhabs mdzes pa dang/ u tpa la dang/ dul gnas me tog dang/ spobs pa mtha' yas 'od zer dang/ drang srong gsal ba dang/ yon tan brtson 'grus dang/ snying po dang/ lha'i bdag po dang/ dgyes pa
Jagadmati, Priyamgama, Caraṇābhijāta, Utpala, Puspadamasthita, Anantapratibhānaraśmi, Rṣiprasanna, Guṇavīrya, Sāra, Marudadhipa, rin chen mthon po dang/ gsal ba dang/ skal ldan shing rta can dang/ bsod nams blo gros dang/ sbyin sreg 'od 'phro dang/ yon tan mtha' yas gzi brjid phung po dang/ seng ge'i stabs dang/ mi g-yo ba dang/ rab gsal dang/ 'od spyod dang/
Uccaratna, Prasanna, Bhāgīrathin, Punyamati, Hutārci, Anantaguṇatejorāśi, Simhavikrāmin, Acala, Prasanna, Cīrṇaprabha, klu dbyangs dang/ 'khor lo 'dzin dang/ dbyig gi mchog dang/ 'jig rten las dga' ba dang/ chos zla dang/ dga' ba mtha' yas grags pa dang/ sprin gyi tog dang/ shes rab 'gros dang/ spos bzang dang/ nam mkha'i dbyangs dang/
Nāgaruta, Cakradhara, Vasuśreṣṭha, Lokapriya, Dharmacandra, Anantaratnakīrti, Meghadhvaja, Prajñāgati, Sugandha, Gaganasvara, lha dang/ lha'i rgyal ba dang/ nor bu gsal dang/ nor bzangs dang/ sgron ma dang/ rin chen nga ro'i dbyangs dang/ skye dbang rgyal po dang/ sgra gcan sbed dang/ dga' ba mdzad pa dang/ seng ge'i blo gros dang/
Simhamati, mi g-yo ba dang/ rin chen grags pa dang/ don mdzad dang/ mdzad pa mtha' gzigs pa dang/ srid pa'i me tog dang mdzod spu ldan dang/ spobs pa mtshungs pa med pa'i rgyal po dang/ ye shes rnam par 'byed pa'i nga ro dang/ seng ge'i mche ba dang/ mdzes par gshegs pa dang/ gsal ] D; gsal pa P. mdzad ра mtha' gzigs pa ] em.; mdzad pa mtha' yas gzigs pa DP; cf. mdzad mtha' gzigs
Bhadrakalpika bsod nams sgron ma dang/ bkra shis Idan dang/ yul 'khor mya ngan med pa dang/ blo gros sems dpa' dang/ blo dang Idan pa dang/ chos kyi sgron ma'i spyan dang/ shin tu gzigs dang/ shugs kyi 'od 'dor ba dang/ stobs can dang/ shes rab me tog dang/
Matimat, Dharmapradīpākṣa, Sudarśana, Vegajaha, Prajñāpuspa, brtan pa'i dbyangs dang/ bde ldan dang/ don gsungs dang/ dgyes par gsal ba dang/ seng ge'i zhal dang/ gtsug phud dang snang mdzad rnams te/ thams cad phyag rgya sna tshogs mdzad pa'o//
Harivaktra, Cūda, /'khor lo 'di ni gru bzhi ste//
/sprul pa'i sku ni 'du shes so/
/ri mo lnga ni me long sogs/
/thams cad mtshan nyid kyis mtshan ma'o//
/brgyad gnyis lha mos byas pa yis/
/mchod pa'i dkyil 'khor bzhi pa'o/ → Dākārṇava, 15.225–226b.
/phyi rol dur khrod brgyad rnams te//
/sgo skyong ma ni rim ji bzhin/
/shar du chu skyes dkar mo sgom//
/byang du ga ndha ri Blo gros sems dpa' for Maticintin. cf. blo sems Bhadrakalpika (D 94, 101v4).
Shugs kyi 'od 'dor ba for Vegajaha. cf. shugs 'dor Bhadrakalpika (D 94, 101v5).
Stobs can for Atibala. He is stobs chen in the Bhadrakalpika (D 94, 101v5).

par gtso bo'i thugs kar nyi ma la gnas pa'i hūm gi sa bon ljang sngon bsam par bya'omgo bor zla ba dang 'khor lo la gnas pa'i om dkar po'o//

de nas gtso bo'i thugs ka’i hūm gnyis kyi bar du ram dmar po las nyi maʼi dkyil 'khor ro//
de'i steng du/ om ha vam hā vām hi vim hī vīm hu vum hū vūm he vem hai vaim ho vom hau vaum ham vam haḥ vaḥ hūm hūm hūm hūm phaṭ phat phat phat svāhā svāhā/
zhes bya ba kha dog dkar dmar g-yon skor du sbrul 'khyil ba ltar gnas pa rdo rje sems dpa' dang phag mo'i ngo bo//
om ha vam hā vām hi vim hī vīm hu vum hū vūm he vem hai vaim ho vom hau vaum ham vam haḥ vaḥ, hūm hūm hūm hūm.
phat phat phat phat, svāhā svāhā” mgo bor de bzhin du nyi ma la/ om na ham nā hām ni him nī hīm nu hum nū hūm ne hem nai haim no hom nau haum nam ham nah haḥ hum hum hum hum phat phat phat phaṭ svāhā svāhā/
ser zhing sngo bas rnam par snang mdzad do//
gshin rje ma'i rang bzhin no//
om na ham nā hām ni him nī hīm nu hum nū hūm ne hem nai haim no hom nau haum nam ham naḥ haḥ om ha vam hā vām hi vim hī vīm hu vum hū vūm he vem hai vaim ho vom hau vaum g-yon skor du na ham no hom nau haum gshin rje ma'i hūm hūm hūm hūm.
phat phat phat phaṭ, svāhā svāhā”;
spyi gtsug tu nyi ma la/ om sva hram svā hrām svi hrim svi hrīm svu hrum svūm hrūm sve hrem svai hraim svo hrom svau hraum svam hram svaḥ hraḥ hūm hūm hūm hūm phat phat phat phaṭ svāhā svāhā/
zhes bya ba dmar zhing dkar ba pa dma gar gyi dbang phyug dang rmongs byed ma'i ngo bo'i//
svā hrām svi hrim svi hrīm svu hrum svūm hrūm sve hrem svai hraim svo hrom svau hraum svam hram svaḥ hraḥ hūm hūm hūm hūm.
phaṭ phat phat phat, svāhā svāhā”;
dpung pa gnyis kyi nang du nyi ma la/ om va hram vā hrām vi hrim vī hrīm vu hrum vū hrūm ve hrem vai hraim vo hrom vau hraum vam hram vaḥ hraḥ hum hum hūm hūm phat phat phat phat svāhā svāhā/
zhes bya ba gnag cing ser ba he ru ka dang skyod byed ma'o//
om va hram vā hrām vi hrim vī hrīm vu hrum vū hrūm ve hrem vai hraim vo hrom vau hraum vam hram vaḥ hraḥ hūm hūm hūm hūm phaṭ phat phaṭ phaṭ svāhā svāhā;
spyan gnyis pa la nyi ma'i steng du/ om hūm hūm ham ham hām hām him him hīm hīm hum hum hūm hūm hem hem haim haim hom hom haum haum ham ham hah haḥ hūm hūm hūm hūm phat phat phat phat svāhā svāhā/

svūm hrūm svo hrom svau hraum svam hram svaḥ hraḥ vam hram skyod byed ma'o om hum hūm hum hum hūm hūm hem hem om hūm hūm ham ham hām hām him him hīm hīm hum hum hūm hūm hem hem haim haim hom hom haum haum ham ham hah haḥ hūm hūm hūm hūm.
